Note 16 (2026/06/09 12:56, RS山口): - The full specification (final version): - "1商品1000点以上購入した売上は返品できないようにする。" (Make sales with 1000+ items of one product non-returnable.) - 対象 (Target): 1か月以内の返品、部分返品 (Return within 1 month, partial return) - 動き (Behavior): 該当売上で返品を実施すると黄色POPでメッセージ (When returning on the applicable sale, show a message with a yellow POP) - メッセージ (Message - updated wording): "1商品1000点以上購入を含むレシートは「1か月以内の返品」はできません。「返品」から返品を行ってください。" (Receipts containing purchases of 1000+ items of a single product cannot use "return within 1 month". Please perform the return from "返品" (return).)

13	    /// <summary>
14	    /// 取引復元用クラス
15	    /// </summary>
16	    [Serializable]
17	    public class ReadReceiptObject
18	    {
19	        /// <summary>
20	        /// 入力日付フォーマット
21	        /// </summary>
22	        private const string DateFormat = "yyyyMMdd";
23	
24	        /// <summary>
25	        /// 入力した日付
26	        /// </summary>
27	        public DateTime InputDate { get; private set; }
28	
29	        /// <summary>
30	        /// 取消対象端末番号を取得します。
31	        /// </summary>
32	        public int InputTerminalNo { get; private set; }
33	
34	        /// <summary>
35	        /// 入力されたレシート番号を取得します。
36	        /// </summary>
37	        public long InputReceiptNo { get; private set; }
38	
39	        /// <summary>
40	        /// 取消対象取引インスタンスを取得する
41	        /// </summary>
42	        public TranDataSet TranDataSet { get; private set; }
43	
44	        /// <summary>
45	        /// 取消したレシートデータ
46	        /// </summary>
47	        public string ReceiptData { get; private set; }
48	
49	        /// <summary>
50	        /// データ更新が必要かを取得します。
51	        /// </summary>
52	        public bool TempNeedRefresh { get; private set; }
53	
54	        /// <summary>
55	        /// 領収書発行済みか
56	        /// </summary>
57	        public bool IsEvidenceReceiptIssued { get; private set; }
58	
59	        /// <summary>
60	        /// バーコードをスキャンする
61	        /// </summary>
62	        /// <param name="inputData">入力値</param>
63	        /// <param name="userData">ユーザー情報</param>
64	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
65	        public bool ScanReceiptBarcode(string inputData, UserData userData)
66	        {
67	            DateTime date;
68	            int terminalNo;
69	            long receiptNo;
70	
71	            int startIndex = 0;
72	            int length = DateFormat.Length;
73	            if (!this.VerifyInputDate(userData, inputData.Substring(startIndex, length), out date))
74	            {
75	                return false;
76	            }
77	
78	            startIndex += length;
79	            length = userData.StoreCode.Length;
80	            if (!this.VerifyStoreCode(userData, inputData.Substring(startIndex, length)))
81	            {
82	                return false;
83	            }
84	
85	            startIndex += length;
86	            length = 4;
87	            if (!this.VerifyInputTerminalNo(userData, inputData.Substring(startIndex, length), out terminalNo))
88	            {
89	                return false;
90	            }
91	
92	            startIndex += length;
93	            length = BusinessCounter.GetLength(userData.DataAccessParameter, CounterCodes.ReceiptNo);
94	            if (!this.VerifyInputReceiptNo(userData, inputData.Substring(startIndex, length), out receiptNo))
95	            {
96	                return false;
97	            }
98	
99	            this.InputDate = date;
100	            this.InputTerminalNo = terminalNo;
101	            this.InputReceiptNo = receiptNo;
102	
103	            this.TempNeedRefresh = true;
104	            return true;
105	        }
106	
107	        /// <summary>
108	        /// 取引データセットを読み込みます。
109	        /// </summary>
110	        /// <param name="userData">ユーザーデータ</param>
111	        /// <param name="inputDate">入力日</param>
112	        /// <param name="inputTerminalNo">入力端末番号</param>
113	        /// <param name="inputReceiptNo">入力レシート番号</param>
114	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
115	        public bool ReadTranDataSet(UserData userData, string inputDate, string inputTerminalNo, string inputReceiptNo)
116	        {
117	            DateTime date;
118	            int terminalNo;
119	            long receiptNo;
120	            if (!this.VerifyInputDate(userData, inputDate, out date))
121	            {
122	                return false;
123	            }
124	
125	            if (!this.VerifyInputTerminalNo(userData, inputTerminalNo, out terminalNo))
126	            {
127	                return false;
128	            }
129	
130	            if (!this.VerifyInputReceiptNo(userData, inputReceiptNo, out receiptNo))
131	            {
132	                return false;
133	            }
134	
135	            var rsTranLog = this.GetTranDataForRestore(userData, terminalNo, receiptNo, date);
136	            if (rsTranLog == null || !rsTranLog.IsSuccess)
137	            {
138	                return false;
139	            }
140	
141	            if (rsTranLog.IsVoided)
142	            {
143	                return false;
144	            }
145	
146	            TranDataSet tranDs = new TranDataSet();
147	            tranDs.ReadXmlEx(rsTranLog.TransactionData);
148	            this.TranDataSet = tranDs;
149	            this.ReceiptData = rsTranLog.ReceiptData;
150	            this.InputDate = date;
151	            this.InputTerminalNo = terminalNo;
152	            this.InputReceiptNo = receiptNo;
153	            this.IsEvidenceReceiptIssued = rsTranLog.IsEvidenceReceiptIssued;
154	            return true;
155	        }
156	
157	        /// <summary>
158	        /// 取引データセットを読み込みます。
159	        /// </summary>
160	        /// <param name="userData">ユーザーデータ</param>
161	        /// <param name="inputDate">入力日</param>
162	        /// <param name="inputTerminalNo">入力端末番号</param>
163	        /// <param name="inputReceiptNo">入力レシート番号</param>
164	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
165	        public bool LocalReadTranDataSet(UserData userData, string inputDate, string inputTerminalNo, string inputReceiptNo)
166	        {
167	            DateTime date;
168	            int terminalNo;
169	            long receiptNo;
170	            if (!this.VerifyInputDate(userData, inputDate, out date))
171	            {
172	                return false;
173	            }
174	
175	            if (!this.VerifyInputTerminalNo(userData, inputTerminalNo, out terminalNo))
176	            {
177	                return false;
178	            }
179	
180	            if (!this.VerifyInputReceiptNo(userData, inputReceiptNo, out receiptNo))
181	            {
182	                return false;
183	            }
184	
185	            var rsTranLog = this.LocalGetTranDataForRestore(userData, terminalNo, receiptNo, date);
186	            if (rsTranLog == null || !rsTranLog.IsSuccess)
187	            {
188	                return false;
189	            }
190	
191	            if (rsTranLog.IsVoided)
192	            {
193	                return false;
194	            }
195	
196	            TranDataSet tranDs = new TranDataSet();
197	            tranDs.ReadXmlEx(rsTranLog.TransactionData);
198	            this.TranDataSet = tranDs;
199	            this.ReceiptData = rsTranLog.ReceiptData;
200	            this.InputDate = date;
201	            this.InputTerminalNo = terminalNo;
202	            this.InputReceiptNo = receiptNo;
203	            this.IsEvidenceReceiptIssued = rsTranLog.IsEvidenceReceiptIssued;
204	            return true;
205	        }
206	
207	        /// <summary>
208	        /// 一時的な値をクリアします。
209	        /// </summary>
210	        public void ClearTempValues()
211	        {
212	            this.TempNeedRefresh = false;
213	        }

215	        /// <summary>
216	        /// 年月日入力検証
217	        /// </summary>
218	        /// <param name="userData">ユーザー情報</param>
219	        /// <param name="input">入力値</param>
220	        /// <param name="date">年月日</param>
221	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
222	        private bool VerifyInputDate(UserData userData, string input, out DateTime date)
223	        {
224	            date = default(DateTime);
225	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input))
226	            {
227	                return false;
228	            }
229	
230	            if (!DateTime.TryParseExact(input, DateFormat, null, DateTimeStyles.None, out date))
231	            {
232	                return false;
233	            }
234	
235	            DateTime limitDate = DateTime.Now.Date.AddMonths(-1);
236	            if (date < limitDate)
237	            {
238	                // １ヵ月以上の取消は出来ないです。
239	                return false;
240	            }
241	
242	            return true;
243	        }
244	
245	        /// <summary>
246	        /// 店舗コード検証
247	        /// </summary>
248	        /// <param name="userData">ユーザー情報</param>
249	        /// <param name="input">入力値</param>
250	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
251	        private bool VerifyStoreCode(UserData userData, string input)
252	        {
253	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input))
254	            {
255	                return false;
256	            }
257	
258	            // 自店舗以外は不可
259	            string ownStoreCode = userData.StoreCode;
260	            if (ownStoreCode != input)
261	            {
262	                return false;
263	            }
264	
265	            return true;
266	        }
267	
268	        /// <summary>
269	        /// 端末番号入力検証
270	        /// </summary>
271	        /// <param name="userData">ユーザー情報</param>
272	        /// <param name="input">入力値</param>
273	        /// <param name="terminalNo">端末番号</param>
274	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
275	        private bool VerifyInputTerminalNo(UserData userData, string input, out int terminalNo)
276	        {
277	            terminalNo = 0;
278	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input))
279	            {
280	                return false;
281	            }
282	
283	            if (!int.TryParse(input, out terminalNo))
284	            {
285	                return false;
286	            }
287	
288	            return true;
289	        }
290	
291	        /// <summary>
292	        /// レシート番号入力検証
293	        /// </summary>
294	        /// <param name="userData">ユーザー情報</param>
295	        /// <param name="input">入力値</param>
296	        /// <param name="receiptNo">レシート番号</param>
297	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
298	        private bool VerifyInputReceiptNo(UserData userData, string input, out long receiptNo)
299	        {
300	            receiptNo = 0;
301	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input))
302	            {
303	                return false;
304	            }
305	
306	            if (!long.TryParse(input, out receiptNo))
307	            {
308	                return false;
309	            }
310	
311	            long receiptStartNo = BusinessCounter.GetStartNumber(userData.DataAccessParameter, CounterCodes.ReceiptNo);
312	            long receiptEndNo = BusinessCounter.GetEndNumber(userData.DataAccessParameter, CounterCodes.ReceiptNo);
313	            if (receiptNo < receiptStartNo || receiptNo > receiptEndNo)
314	            {
315	                return false;
316	            }
317	
318	            return true;
319	        }
320	
321	        /// <summary>
322	        /// 復元の対象となる取引データを取得する
323	        /// </summary>
324	        /// <param name="userData">ユーザー情報</param>
325	        /// <param name="terminalNo">
326	        /// 端末番号
327	        /// </param>
328	        /// <param name="receiptNo">
329	        /// レシートNo.
330	        /// </param>
331	        /// <param name="generateDateTime">
332	        /// 作成年月日
333	        /// </param>
334	        /// <returns>
335	        /// 取引データ
336	        /// </returns>
337	        private GetTransactionLogResult GetTranDataForRestore(UserData userData, int terminalNo, long receiptNo, DateTime generateDateTime)
338	        {
339	            var row = TransactionLogAccessor.GetTLogManagementRow(
340	                userData.DataAccessParameter,
341	                terminalNo,
342	                receiptNo,
343	                generateDateTime.Date);
344	
345	            if (row != null && !row.IsTransactionDataNull())
346	            {
347	                GetTransactionLogResult result = new GetTransactionLogResult();
348	                result.IsSuccess = true;
349	                result.TransactionData = row.TransactionData;
350	                result.ReceiptData = !row.IsReceiptDataNull() ? row.ReceiptData : null;
351	                result.IsVoided = !row.IsIsVoidedNull() ? row.IsVoided : false;
352	                result.IsEvidenceReceiptIssued = !row.IsIsEvidenceReceiptIssuedNull() ? row.IsEvidenceReceiptIssued : false;
353	                return result;
354	            }
355	 
356	            var deviceManager = Factory.CreatePlugin(FrameworkPluginIds.DeviceManager);
357	            var logicServiceClient = deviceManager.GetDevice(DeviceIds.LogicServiceClient);
358	
359	            if (logicServiceClient != null)
360	            {
361	                var rsLogicService = logicServiceClient.GetTransactionLog(userData.DataAccessParameter, generateDateTime, terminalNo, receiptNo);
362	
363	                if (rsLogicService.IsSuccess && rsLogicService.Value.IsSuccess)
364	                {
365	                    return rsLogicService.Value;
366	                }
367	            }
368	
369	            return null;
370	        }
371	
372	        /// <summary>
373	        /// 復元の対象となる取引データを取得する
374	        /// </summary>
375	        /// <param name="userData">ユーザー情報</param>
376	        /// <param name="terminalNo">
377	        /// 端末番号
378	        /// </param>
379	        /// <param name="receiptNo">
380	        /// レシートNo.
381	        /// </param>
382	        /// <param name="generateDateTime">
383	        /// 作成年月日
384	        /// </param>
385	        /// <returns>
386	        /// 取引データ
387	        /// </returns>
388	        private GetTransactionLogResult LocalGetTranDataForRestore(UserData userData, int terminalNo, long receiptNo, DateTime generateDateTime)
389	        {
390	            // ローカル一括取消と横向き領収証はAZUREから取得する
391	            var deviceManager = Factory.CreatePlugin(FrameworkPluginIds.DeviceManager);
392	            var logicServiceClient = deviceManager.GetDevice(DeviceIds.LogicServiceClient);
393	
394	            if (logicServiceClient != null)
395	            {
396	                var rsLogicService = logicServiceClient.GetTransactionLog(userData.DataAccessParameter, generateDateTime, terminalNo, receiptNo);
397	
398	                if (rsLogicService.IsSuccess && rsLogicService.Value.IsSuccess)
399	                {
400	                    return rsLogicService.Value;
401	                }
402	            }
403	
404	            return null;
405	        }
406	    }

14	namespace ForYouApplications.POS4U.Business.ReSales
15	{
16	    /// <summary>
17	    /// 横向き領収書取引
18	    /// </summary>
19	    [Serializable]
20	    public class EvidenceReceiptTran : SalesTran
21	    {
22	        /// <summary>
23	        /// コンストラクタ
24	        /// </summary>
25	        public EvidenceReceiptTran()
26	        {
27	            this.CurrentState = EvidenceReceiptTranStates.Neutral;
28	        }
29	
30	        /// <summary>
31	        /// 元売上商品明細情報
32	        /// </summary>
33	        public Dictionary<int, TranDataSet.LineItemRow> LineItemDataSeDataSet { get; set; } = new Dictionary<int, TranDataSet.LineItemRow>();
34	
35	        /// <summary>
36	        /// 取引タイプを取得します。
37	        /// </summary>
38	        public override TranType TranType
39	        {
40	            get { return TranTypes.EvidenceReceipt; }
41	        }
42	
43	        /// <summary>
44	        /// 取引ログ種別を取得します。
45	        /// </summary>
46	        public override TranLogType TranLogType
47	        {
48	            get
49	            {
50	                if (this.CurrentState == EvidenceReceiptTranStates.WaitingRecoverConfirm)
51	                {
52	                    return !this.UserData.IsTrainingMode ? TranLogTypes.NormalEvidenceReceipt : TranLogTypes.TrainingEvidenceReceipt;
53	                }
54	
55	                return TranLogTypes.None;
56	            }
57	        }
58	
59	        /// <summary>
60	        /// 領収書発行種別
61	        /// </summary>
62	        public EvidenceReceiptType EvidenceReceiptType { get; set; } = EvidenceReceiptTypes.None;
63	
64	        /// <summary>
65	        /// レシート呼出し管理用オブジェクト
66	        /// </summary>
67	        public ReadReceiptObject ReadReceiptObject { get; set; } = new ReadReceiptObject();
68	
69	        /// <summary>
70	        /// レシートのリスト呼出し管理用オブジェクト
71	        /// </summary>
72	        public List<ReadReceiptObject> ReadReceiptObjects { get; } = new List<ReadReceiptObject>();
73	
74	        /// <summary>
75	        /// 取引金額
76	        /// </summary>
77	        public decimal Amount
78	        {
79	            get
80	            {
81	                decimal totalAmount = 0;
82	                if (this.LineItems.Items.Count != 0)
83	                {
84	                    foreach (var lineItem in this.LineItems.Items.Where(l => l.LineItemState == LineItemStates.Fixed))
85	                    {
86	                        var lineItemOriginal = this.LineItemDataSeDataSet.Where(i => i.Key == lineItem.KeyNo).FirstOrDefault().Value;
87	
88	                        bool isIncludedTax = lineItem.GetTaxType() == TaxTypes.IncludedTax.Code;
89	                        if (!isIncludedTax)
90	                        {
91	                            totalAmount += ((lineItemOriginal.Amount + lineItemOriginal.TaxDivided) / lineItemOriginal.Quantity) * lineItem.Quantity;
92	                        }
93	                        else
94	                        {
95	                            totalAmount += (lineItemOriginal.Amount / lineItemOriginal.Quantity) * lineItem.Quantity;
96	                        }
97	                    }
98	
99	                    return totalAmount;
100	                }
101	              
102	                foreach (var readReceiptObject in this.ReadReceiptObjects)
103	                {
104	                    totalAmount += readReceiptObject.TranDataSet.SalesHeader[0].TotalAmountWithTaxes;
105	                }
106	
107	                return totalAmount;
108	            }
109	        }
110	
111	        /// <summary>
112	        /// 取引消費税
113	        /// </summary>
114	        public decimal TaxesAmount
115	        {
116	            get
117	            {
118	                decimal taxesAmount = 0;
119	
120	                if (this.LineItems.Items.Count != 0)
121	                {
122	                    foreach (var lineItem in this.LineItems.Items.Where(l => l.LineItemState == LineItemStates.Fixed))
123	                    {
124	                        var lineItemOriginal = this.LineItemDataSeDataSet.Where(i => i.Key == lineItem.KeyNo).FirstOrDefault().Value;
125	                        taxesAmount += (lineItemOriginal.TaxDivided / lineItemOriginal.Quantity) * lineItem.Quantity;
126	                    }
127	
128	                    return taxesAmount;
129	                }
130	                
131	                foreach (var readReceiptObject in this.ReadReceiptObjects)
132	                {
133	                    taxesAmount += readReceiptObject.TranDataSet.SalesHeader[0].TaxesAmount;
134	                }
135	                
136	                return taxesAmount;
137	            }
138	        }
139	
140	        /// <summary>
141	        /// バーコードをスキャンする
142	        /// </summary>
143	        /// <param name="inputData">入力値</param>
144	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
145	        public bool ScanReceiptBarcode(string inputData)
146	        {
147	            if (EvidenceReceiptTranStates.Neutral != this.CurrentState
148	                && EvidenceReceiptTranStates.SumReceiptScan != this.CurrentState)
149	            {
150	                return false;
151	            }
152	
153	            return this.ReadReceiptObject.ScanReceiptBarcode(inputData, this.UserData);
154	        }
155	
156	        /// <summary>
157	        /// 取引復元を行います。
158	        /// </summary>
159	        /// <returns>true:正常/false:異常</returns>
160	        public bool RestoreEvidenceItems()
161	        {
162	            var restoreTranObj = new RestoreTranObject();
163	            if (!restoreTranObj.RestoreEvidenceTran(this, this.ReadReceiptObjects.LastOrDefault().TranDataSet))
164	            {
165	                return false;
166	            }
167	
168	            int keyNo = 1;
169	            for (int lineItemNo = 1; lineItemNo <= this.ReadReceiptObject.TranDataSet.LineItem.Count; lineItemNo++)
170	            {
171	                var lineItemData = ReadReceiptObject.TranDataSet.LineItem.Where(x => x.LineItemNo == lineItemNo).First();
172	                if (!lineItemData.IsCanceled)
173	                {
174	                    this.LineItemDataSeDataSet.Add(keyNo, lineItemData);
175	                    keyNo++;
176	                }
177	            }
178	
179	            return true;
180	        }
181	
182	        /// <summary>
183	        /// 対象の明細の数量変更が可能かを返します。
184	        /// </summary>
185	        /// <param name="lineItem">対象の明細</param>
186	        /// <param name="quantity">新数量</param>
187	        /// <returns>true:変更可/false:変更不可</returns>
188	        public override bool CanChangeQuantity(LineItemBase lineItem, int quantity)
189	        {
190	            int lineItemNo = 1;
191	
192	            for (int keyNo = 1; keyNo < this.ReadReceiptObjects.LastOrDefault().TranDataSet.LineItem.Count + 1; keyNo++)
193	            {
194	                var lineItemData = this.ReadReceiptObjects.LastOrDefault().TranDataSet.LineItem.Where(x => x.LineItemNo == keyNo).First();
195	                if (lineItemData.IsCanceled)
196	                {
197	                    continue;
198	                }
199	
200	                if (lineItemNo == lineItem.KeyNo)
201	                {
202	                    if (lineItemData.Quantity < quantity)
203	                    {
204	                        this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotAddQuantity);
205	                        return false;
206	                    }
207	                }
208	
209	                lineItemNo++;
210	            }
211	
212	            return true;
213	        }
214	
215	        /// <summary>
216	        /// レシート読み込み
217	        /// </summary>
218	        /// <param name="inputDate">入力日</param>
219	        /// <param name="inputTerminalNo">入力端末番号</param>
220	        /// <param name="inputReceiptNo">入力レシート番号</param>
221	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
222	        public bool ReadTranDataSet(string inputDate, string inputTerminalNo, string inputReceiptNo)
223	        {
224	            if (EvidenceReceiptTranStates.Neutral != this.CurrentState
225	                && EvidenceReceiptTranStates.SumReceiptScan != this.CurrentState)
226	            {
227	                return false;
228	            }
229	
230	            var readReceiptObject = new ReadReceiptObject();
231	
232	            if (!readReceiptObject.LocalReadTranDataSet(this.UserData, inputDate, inputTerminalNo, inputReceiptNo))
233	            {
234	                // 取引が存在しないため領収書発行不可
235	                this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorNotFoundSalesEvidenceTransaction);
236	                return false;
237	            }
238	
239	            foreach (var r in ReadReceiptObjects)
240	            {
241	                if (r.InputDate == readReceiptObject.InputDate
242	                    && r.InputReceiptNo == readReceiptObject.InputReceiptNo
243	                    && r.InputTerminalNo == readReceiptObject.InputTerminalNo)
244	                {
245	                    // 二重発行チェック
246	                    this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorNotFoundSalesEvidenceTransaction);
247	                    return false;
248	                }
249	            }
250	
251	            if (readReceiptObject.IsEvidenceReceiptIssued)
252	            {
253	                // 二重発行チェック
254	                this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorNotFoundSalesEvidenceTransaction);
255	                return false;
256	            }
257	
258	            var tranDs = readReceiptObject.TranDataSet;
259	            int transactionType = tranDs.TransactionHeader[0].TransactionType;
260	
261	            if (transactionType != TranLogTypes.NormalSales.Number && transactionType != TranLogTypes.TrainingSales.Number)
262	            {
263	                // 売上以外は領収書発行不可
264	                this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotSalesEvidenceTransaction);
265	                return false;
266	            }
267	            else if ((this.UserData.IsTrainingMode && transactionType == TranLogTypes.NormalSales.Number)
268	                || (!this.UserData.IsTrainingMode && transactionType == TranLogTypes.TrainingSales.Number))
269	            {
270	                // モードが異なる売上取引は領収書発行不可
271	                this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotVoidCurrentMode);
272	                return false;
273	            }
274	
275	            this.ReadReceiptObject = CopyUtility.DeepCopy(readReceiptObject);
276	            this.ReadReceiptObjects.Add(readReceiptObject);
277	
278	            return true;
279	        }
280	
281	        /// <summary>
282	        /// 取引中止
283	        /// </summary>
284	        /// <returns>true:成功/false:失敗</returns>
285	        public override bool CancelTran()
286	        {
287	            this.CurrentState = EvidenceReceiptTranStates.Canceled;
288	            return true;
289	        }
290	
291	        /// <summary>
292	        /// 取引終了処理
293	        /// </summary>
294	        /// <returns>true:正常/false:異常</returns>
295	        public override bool EndTran()
296	        {
297	            var printer = Factory.CreatePlugin(FrameworkPluginIds.DeviceManager).GetDevice(DeviceIds.POSPrinter);
298	
299	            if (printer == null || !printer.IsEnable)
300	            {
301	                return false;
302	            }
303	
304	            // データ挿入
305	            if (this.CurrentState == EvidenceReceiptTranStates.SumReceiptConfirm)
306	            {
307	                this.CurrentState = EvidenceReceiptTranStates.WaitingRecoverConfirm;
308	                this.FixSumTran();
309	                return true;
310	            }
311	
312	            this.CurrentState = EvidenceReceiptTranStates.WaitingRecoverConfirm;
313	            this.FixTran();
314	
315	            return true;
316	        }
317	
318	        /// <summary>
319	        /// レシートのリストのクリア処理
320	        /// </summary>
321	        public void ClearReadReceiptObjects()
322	        {
323	            this.ReadReceiptObjects.Clear();
324	        }

327	        /// 一時値のクリア処理
328	        /// </summary>

330	        {
331	            this.ReadReceiptObject.ClearTempValues();
332	        }

  1. EvidenceReceiptTran = 横向き領収書取引 (lateral/sideways receipt transaction) = "証憑レシート" (evidence receipt). This is for issuing a receipt (領収書) for a past transaction, NOT for returning goods. So this is the #4900 related issue.

  2. ReadReceiptObject.ReadTranDataSet / LocalReadTranDataSet = reads the past transaction data by date/terminal/receipt number. Key logic:

  3. Line 235: DateTime limitDate = DateTime.Now.Date.AddMonths(-1); - if date < limitDate, returns false (1 month limit). This is the "1か月以内の返品" rule!

  4. The transaction data is stored as XML (rsTranLog.TransactionData), read via tranDs.ReadXmlEx(...).

  5. The key method GetTranDataForRestore gets the transaction log row, and the transaction data is stored as a string (XML serialized TranDataSet).

398	            else if (reSalesType == ReSalesTypes.ReSales)
399	            {

410	                {
411	                    this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotReSalesDuetoCreditCombinedUse);
412	                    return false;
413	                }
414

429	                        if (tranDs.Member.Count > 0)

431	                            // 商品一つの場合、一括取消利用
432	                            this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotReSalesDuetoItemCount);
433	                            return false;
434	                        }

436	                }

438

440	            {

442	                var member = tranDs.Member[0];

453	                };

457	                bool continueWithValueNotFound = isPointOnlyInquiry ||
458	                                                  (!salesHeader.IsOfflinePointCardNoNull() &&
459	                                                   !string.IsNullOrEmpty(salesHeader.OfflinePointCardNo) &&
460	                                                   !member.IsCustomerIDInputTypeNull() &&
461	                                                   !string.IsNullOrEmpty(member.CustomerIDInputType) &&
462	                                                   member.CustomerIDInputType == CustomerIDInputTypes.OneTimeBarcode.Code);
463	
464	                if (!this.MemberObject.Inquiry(member.PointCardNo, null, bool.FalseString, now, this.BusinessDate, false, continueWithValueNotFound, isPointOnlyInquiry))
465	                {
466	                    this.MemberObject.ClearFixedData();
467	
468	                    // 会員情報が取得できない場合は、一括取消出来ない。
469	                    return false;
470	                }
471	            }
472	
473	            this.CurrentState = VoidTranStates.SelectReason;
474	
475	            return true;
476	        }
477	
478	        /// <summary>
479	        /// 返金支払いを追加します。
480	        /// </summary>
481	        /// <param name="transferabletLineNos">現金振替支払い</param>
482	        /// <returns>true:正常/false:異常</returns>
483	        public bool AddVoidPayments(string[] transferabletLineNos)
484	        {
485	            if (this.PaymentObject.Items.Count > 0)
486	            {
487	                if (!this.PaymentObject.CancelPayments())
488	                {
489	                    return false;
490	                }
491	            }
492	
493	            this.PaymentObject.PaymentParameter = this.CreatePaymentParameter();
494	
495	            // 券支払い行番号と選択枚数
496	            List<CashTransferParam> lineNosList = new List<CashTransferParam>();
497	            if (transferabletLineNos != null)
498	            {
499	                lineNosList = transferabletLineNos.Where(s => s.Contains("&")).Select(item => new CashTransferParam
500	                {
501	                    LineNo = int.Parse(item.Split('&')[0]),
502	                    TicketCount = int.Parse(item.Split('&')[1])
503	                }).ToList();
504	            }
505	
506	            var tranDs = this.ReadReceiptObject.TranDataSet;
507	            var salesHeader = tranDs.SalesHeader[0];
508	
509	            // 現金振替の行番号
510	            ArrayList untransferLineNos = new ArrayList();
511	            if (lineNosList != null && lineNosList.Count > 0)
512	            {
513	                foreach (var payment in tranDs.Payment)
514	                {
515	                    if (lineNosList.Where(s => s.LineNo == payment.LineNo && s.TicketCount == 0).FirstOrDefault() != null)
516	                    {
517	                        untransferLineNos.Add(payment.LineNo);
518	                    }
519	                }
520	            }
521	
522	            foreach (var payment in tranDs.Payment)
523	            {
524	                int orginalTicketCount = payment.TicketCount;
525	
526	                // 券支払復元の枚数変更
527	                if (lineNosList != null && lineNosList.Count > 0 && lineNosList.Where(s => s.LineNo == payment.LineNo && s.TicketCount != 0).FirstOrDefault() != null)
528	                {
529	                    payment.TicketCount = lineNosList.Where(s => s.LineNo == payment.LineNo).ToList()[0].TicketCount;
530	                }
531	                
532	                if (!untransferLineNos.Contains(payment.LineNo) && 
533	                    (payment.PaymentCode != PaymentTypes.Cash.Code && 
534	                     payment.PaymentCode != PaymentTypes.CashInput.Code))
535	                {
536	                    if (!this.PaymentObject.RestorePayment(tranDs, payment))
537	                    {
538	                        // ここには入らないはずです。
539	                        Logger.Error($"Error RestorePayment. LineNo = {payment.LineNo}, PaymentCode = {payment.PaymentCode}");
540	
541	                        if (!this.PaymentObject.CancelPayments())
542	                        {
543	                            Logger.Error($"Error CancelPayments.");
544	                        }
545	
546	                        return false;
547	                    }
548	
549	                    payment.TicketCount = orginalTicketCount;
550	                }
551	            }
552	
553	            return true;
554	        }

597	
598	        /// <summary>
599	        /// 取引終了処理
600	        /// </summary>
601	        /// <returns>true:正常/false:異常</returns>
602	        public override bool EndTran()
603	        {
604	            var tranDs = this.ReadReceiptObject.TranDataSet;
605	            var salesHeader = tranDs.SalesHeader[0];
606	
607	            decimal totalAmountWithTaxes = salesHeader.TotalAmountWithTaxes;
608	
609	            if (this.PaymentObject.Items.Count == 0 || this.PaymentObject.PaymentsAmount < totalAmountWithTaxes)
610	            {
611	                return false;
612	            }
613	
614	            var hasCAFISArchPayment = (PaymentCAFISArchLANBase)this.PaymentObject.Items.Where(x => x.PaymentType == PaymentTypes.UnionPayLAN).FirstOrDefault();
615	            var cafisArchResult = hasCAFISArchPayment?.CAFISArchResult;
616	            try
617	            {
618	                this.GenerateDateTime = DateTimeUtility.GetUserTimeNow();
619	                
620	                if (this.IsNeedUpdatePoint && (hasCAFISArchPayment == null || cafisArchResult == null))
621	                {
622	                    // ポイント問合せ（排他あり）
623	                    if (!this.MemberObject.LockInquiry(
624	                            this.PaymentObject.GetPointPaymentAmount(),
625	                            true,
626	                            this.GenerateDateTime.Value,
627	                            this.BusinessDate,
628	                            this.PaymentObject.PaymentParameter.IsPayIn))
629	                    {
630	                        return false;
631	                    }
632	                }
633	
634	                // 支払いを確定します。
635	                bool rsFixPayments = this.PaymentObject.FixPayments();
636	
637	                if (this.PaymentObject.IsOperating)
638	                {
639	                    this.CurrentState = this.PaymentObject.CurrentPayment.PaymentState;
640	                    return rsFixPayments;
641	                }
642	
643	                if (!rsFixPayments)
644	                {
645	                    return false;
646	                }
647	
648	                decimal totalAmountWithNoTaxes = salesHeader.TotalAmountWithNoTaxes;
649	
650	                // 支払税按分計算
651	                Factory.CreatePlugin(SalesPluginIds.TaxManager).CalcPaymentTaxDevided(this.PaymentObject, totalAmountWithTaxes, totalAmountWithTaxes - totalAmountWithNoTaxes);
652	
653	                if (this.MemberObject.HasMember)
654	                {
655	                    // ポイント計算
656	                    Factory.CreatePlugin(SalesPluginIds.PointManager).CalcOtherTran(this);
657	
658	                    // 排他が取得出来ている場合は、リアルでポイント更新を行う。
659	                    if (this.MemberObject.IsPointLocked)
660	                    {
661	                        decimal includedTax = 0;
662	                        decimal excludedTax = 0;
663	
664	                        foreach (var tax in tranDs.Tax)
665	                        {
666	                            if (tax.TaxType == TaxTypes.IncludedTax.Code)
667	                            {
668	                                includedTax += tax.TaxAmount;
669	                            }
670	                            else if (tax.TaxType == TaxTypes.ExcludedTax.Code)
671	                            {
672	                                excludedTax += tax.TaxAmount;
673	                            }
674	                        }
675	
676	                        // ポイント更新
677	                        if (!this.MemberObject.Update(
678	                                tranDs.Payment.Where(p => p.PaymentCode == PaymentTypes.Point.Code).Sum(p => p.Amount),
679	                                true,
680	                                this.GenerateDateTime.Value,
681	                                this.BusinessDate,
682	                                salesHeader.TotalQuantity,
683	                                totalAmountWithTaxes,
684	                                includedTax,
685	                                excludedTax,
686	                                PointServiceDealDiv.Return,
687	                                this.GetTransactionData()))
688	                        {
689	                            return false;
690	                        }
691	                    }
692	                }
693	
694	                this.CurrentState = VoidTranStates.Fixed;
695	
696	                this.FixTran();
697	
698	                if (this.IsReSales)
699	                {
700	                    // 部分取消の場合、レシート印字一旦保留、お釣り出せない
701	                    var printInfos = this.UserData.GetRJPrintInfoStore();
702	                    printInfos.SetTempPrintInfos(this.ReceiptPrintData);
703	                }
704	                else
705	                {
706	                    this.PaymentObject.DispenseChange();
707	                }
708	
709	                return true;
710	            }
711	            finally
712	            {
713	                if (this.MemberObject.IsPointLocked &&
714	                    (hasCAFISArchPayment == null || cafisArchResult != null))
715	                {
716	                    // ポイント排他解除（成否を問わない）
717	                    this.MemberObject.UnLockUpdate(
718	                        this.GenerateDateTime.Value,
719	                        this.BusinessDate);
720	                }
721	            }
722	        }
723

Schema for findings:

const FINDINGS = {
  type: 'object',
  additionalProperties: false,
  properties: {
    area: { type: 'string', description: 'which hypothesis/area investigated' },
    summary: { type: 'string', description: '2-4 sentence conclusion' },
    isRootCause: { type: 'string', enum: ['yes','likely','no','unclear'] },
    confidence: { type: 'string', enum: ['low','medium','high'] },
    evidence: {
      type: 'array',
      items: {
        type: 'object',
        additionalProperties: false,
        properties: {
          file: { type: 'string' },
          lines: { type: 'string' },
          detail: { type: 'string' }
        },
        required: ['file','lines','detail']
      }
    },
    explainsBoth: { type: 'string', description: 'how this explains BOTH return(#6329) and evidence-receipt(#4900) failing at 1000, or why not' },
    recommendedFixPoint: { type: 'string', description: 'concrete file:method where a fix/check should go, if applicable' }
  },
  required: ['area','summary','isRootCause','confidence','evidence']
}

Synthesis schema:

const REPORT = {
  type: 'object',
  additionalProperties: false,
  properties: {
    rootCause: { type: 'string' },
    rootCauseConfidence: { type: 'string', enum:['low','medium','high'] },
    supportingEvidence: { type: 'array', items: { type:'string' } },
    rejectedHypotheses: { type: 'array', items: { type:'string' } },
    fixLocations: { type: 'array', items: { type:'string' } },
    openQuestions: { type: 'array', items: { type:'string' } }
  },
  required: ['rootCause','rootCauseConfidence','supportingEvidence','fixLocations']
}

Now the script body:

phase('Investigate')
const areas = [ {label, prompt}, ... ]
const findings = await parallel(areas.map(a => () => agent(a.prompt, {label: a.label, phase:'Investigate', schema: FINDINGS})))
const valid = findings.filter(Boolean)
log(`collected ${valid.length} findings`)

phase('Synthesize')
const report = await agent(synthesisPrompt(valid), {label:'synthesize', phase:'Synthesize', schema: REPORT})
return { findings: valid, report }

This is extremely valuable. The #4900 timeline reveals key technical details:

  • 2023/04/06 - Issue created. Symptom: 画面表示が固まる (screen freezes) when issuing item-specified evidence receipt for 1000+ qty.
  • 2023/04/25 (#note at line 309): "取引明細総件数上限と明細別件数上限は一致なら、問題発生しないように運用サポートへ確認中" = "If the total transaction detail count limit and the per-detail count limit are consistent, the problem won't occur — confirming with operations support."

THIS IS THE KEY. There are TWO limits: - 取引明細総件数上限 = total number of transaction details (line items) upper limit - 明細別件数上限 = per-detail count (quantity) upper limit

The problem occurs when these are INCONSISTENT. So there's a limit somewhere (likely 取引明細総件数上限 = total line item count limit) and a per-line quantity. When you have one line with qty 1000, and something expands/processes by quantity, it exceeds the total-detail limit.

  • 2024/05/08 (line 436): "店舗サポートより、1000個未満の購入制限はさけたい。改修案確認中" = "From store support: they want to AVOID a purchase limit of under 1000. Reviewing modification plan." — So they don't want to simply cap purchases at <1000.
  • 2025/09/03 (line 519): "石室さん 改修案確認お願いします" = asking 石室 (the same 日本担当者 as #6329!) to review the modification plan.
  • 2026/05/27 (line 550): "店舗サポートより運用回避「売上時のレシート(領収証)で対応する」とのこと。領収証発行できないように止める。" = "From store support: operational workaround — handle it with the receipt (evidence receipt) at time of sale. Stop [them] from issuing evidence receipts [for these]." — Same decision pattern as #6329: block it.
  • 2026/06/05 (line 581): probably the spec (omitted long line).
  • 2026/06/09 (line 591, 612, 621): status change / final spec.

The crucial technical hint is line 309: there's a "取引明細総件数上限" (total transaction line-item count limit) and "明細別件数上限" (per-line quantity limit). When 1 line has qty 1000, and the system processes/expands by quantity, the total exceeds the limit.

This strongly supports a hypothesis: somewhere, line items are EXPANDED or COUNTED by quantity, and there's a hard limit on total expanded items (or total quantity). The number 1000 is likely the limit. When issuing an evidence receipt or doing a return, the code expands each line item into per-quantity entries (or sums quantities), hitting a 1000-item limit (array, grid, receipt line, DB insert batch, etc.), causing a freeze (#4900) or error (#6329).

Now I have the full picture of #4900 too:

#4900 spec (final, 2026/06/09): Block evidence-receipt issuance for 1000+ qty sales. Target: 領収証、指定領収証、合算領収証 (evidence receipt, specified evidence receipt, combined evidence receipt). Yellow POP message: "1商品1000点以上購入を含むレシートは横向き領収証の発行はできません。売上レシートを領収証としてご利用ください。"

Key technical hints from #4900: 1. Symptom is 画面表示が固まる (screen FREEZES) — not an exception. This points to a performance/loop issue (O(n) or O(n²) over quantity) or a UI binding that chokes. 2. Line 309 (2023/04/25): "取引明細総件数上限と明細別件数上限は一致なら、問題発生しないように" — two limits: total transaction detail count limit (取引明細総件数上限) and per-detail quantity limit (明細別件数上限). Problem occurs when inconsistent. This strongly implies there's a hard cap on the TOTAL number of detail lines (possibly 1000), and when one line has qty 1000, something expands by quantity and exceeds it.

So the most likely root cause mechanism: When the system restores/redisplays a past transaction for return or evidence-receipt issuance, it EXPANDS line items by quantity (one entry per unit) OR there's a per-quantity loop, and there's a hard limit (~1000 total expanded entries) in the data grid / array / receipt / DB layer. With 1 line × 1000 qty, the expansion hits/exceeds 1000 → freeze (#4900) / error (#6329).

Actually, wait. Let me reconsider. The two limits being "一致" (consistent) means: there's a max total line-item count (取引明細総件数上限), say 100 or 200, AND a max per-line quantity (明細別件数上限). Actually no — re-reading: "取引明細総件数上限と明細別件数上限は一致なら、問題発生しない" = "if the total-detail-count-limit and the per-detail-count-limit are EQUAL/consistent, the problem won't happen."

Hmm, this is a bit cryptic. Let me interpret: Maybe the sales entry allows up to N total items (where item count = sum of quantities, 取引明細総件数), and each detail line allows up to M quantity (明細別件数上限). If you can sell 1 line × qty 1000, but the total-items-handling limit elsewhere is lower, inconsistency arises. OR: the per-detail quantity limit during SALE might be e.g. 999, but somehow 1000+ got through, and downstream processing (return/receipt) assumes < 1000.

Actually the cleanest interpretation: There IS a limit somewhere = 1000 (取引明細総件数上限 — total quantity / total expanded detail count). A sale can create a single line with qty 1000+ (per-detail limit allows it or wasn't enforced), but when that sale is later RESTORED for return/evidence-receipt, the restoration expands or iterates by quantity and exceeds the 1000 total limit → freeze/error.

The "screen freezes" for #4900 (UI item-specified evidence receipt — the operator SELECTS items, which means the UI lists each item, possibly expanded by quantity) strongly supports the expand-by-quantity in the UI theory. For #6329 (return), partial return also involves selecting items/quantities.

中间情况报告

① 问题的时间轴（已完成复原）

#6329（主问题：退货失败） - 2024/04/26 发现Bug → 2026/05/27 决定采取运用规避 → 2026/06/05 设计草案 → 2026/06/09 最终设计确定（状态：进行中，进度：1＝设计） - 最终应对：在1个月内退货及部分退货中，对于包含1商品1000点以上的收据，通过黄色POP显示 1商品1000点以上購入を含むレシートは「1か月以内の返品」はできません。「返品」から返品を行ってください。 并禁止退货

#4900（类似问题：发行凭证收据时卡顿） - 2023/04/06 发现Bug。症状是 画面表示が固まる（画面フリーズ） 而非异常。 - 2023/04/25 的重要分析：“为使交易明细总件数上限与各明细件数上限一致，防止问题发生” ← 即“总件数上限”与“按明细（数量）的上限”这两个限制的不一致才是原因。 - 2026/06/09 最终设计确定：对于1商品1000点以上的销售，禁止发行横向收据（領収証・指定領収証・合算領收据），通过黄色POP提示消息。

② 目前代码中的核心结构（已完成特定）

  • 退货主体：VoidTran.cs — ReadTranDataSet（1个月内退货，读取本地TLog→Azure）和 LocalReadTranDataSet（批量取消，仅Azure）
  • 过去交易读取：ReadReceiptObject.cs — 通过 VerifyInputDate 的 AddMonths(-1) 实现1个月限制，并通过 TranDataSet.ReadXmlEx(TransactionData) 反序列化
  • #4900 主体：EvidenceReceiptTran.cs — 存在用 Amount 除以 Quantity 的计算逻辑

③ 根本原因的有力假设（基于 #4900 的线索）

4900 的症状是“画面卡死”，并且在注释中提到了“明细总件数上限”与“各明细件数上限”的不一致。由此推测：在还原/再显示过去交易时，存在按数量扩展明细（1个单位＝1条目）或按数量的循环处理，当总扩展数达到约1000的硬上限时，会导致卡死（#4900）或失败（#6329）。

When a past transaction is LOADED and its line items are re-registered through the trusted restore routine, any restored line whose stored Quantity != 1 is re-fed through `LineItemBase.ChangeQuantity` (Business\\Business.Sales\\LineItem\\LineItemBase.cs:180) -> `LineItemCommonLogic.TryParseQuantity` (Business\\Business.Sales\\LineItem\\LineItemCommonLogic.cs:71). TryParseQuantity rejects quantity > 999 by setting `MessageIds.ErrorItemQuantityMaxOver` (Code 1809, \"入力された数量が最大値を超えています\") and returning false (lines 71-75). ChangeQuantity therefore returns false (LineItemBase.cs:182) without ever assigning Quantity, and `RestoreTranObject.RestoreLineItems` (Business\\Business.Sales\\RestoreTranObject.cs:618-625) aborts the whole restore. A single line of exactly 1000 units is the FIRST quantity that exceeds 999, which is precisely why the boundary is 1000 — there is no literal \"1000\" anywhere in the code; it is the configurable-but-defaulted 999 cap plus the off-by-one. This restore routine is SHARED by both failing features: `RestoreReSales` (partial return / #6329, via ReSalesTran.cs:441 -> RestoreTranObject.cs:112) and `RestoreEvidenceTran` (evidence receipt / #4900, via EvidenceReceiptTran.cs:163 -> RestoreTranObject.cs:215). The no-receipt workaround (レシート無し返品) succeeds because it re-enters items via fresh PLU scan rather than restoring stored line items, so it never passes the large stored quantity through ChangeQuantity/TryParseQuantity. This root cause is corroborated by the team's own Redmine #4900 note #1 (\"1商品最大999個の制御があるので、明細商品の復元が失敗になって...ErrorItemQuantityMaxOver... NullReferenceエラー\"). NOTE on the arithmetic/precision hypothesis (two investigators also floated Amount/Quantity integer/decimal division in EvidenceReceiptTran.cs:91/95/125 as the cause): the underlying numeric types rule it out as the PRIMARY cause — Quantity/TotalQuantity are `int`, Amount/TaxDivided/UnitPrice* are `decimal`, so the division is decimal arithmetic that cannot overflow or produce division-by-zero at 1000. 169	        public virtual bool ChangeQuantity(SalesTran tran, string inputQuantity)
170	        {
171	            // パラメータチェック
172	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(inputQuantity))
173	            {
174	                tran.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorInputNeed);
175	                return false;
176	            }
177	
178	            // Decimal型の数量に変換する
179	            int quantity;
180	            if (!LineItemCommonLogic.TryParseQuantity(tran, inputQuantity, out quantity))
181	            {
182	                return false;
183	            }
184	
185	            // 訂正された行は数量変更不可
186	            if (this.LineItemState == LineItemStates.Canceled)
187	            {
188	                tran.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotChangeQuantityForCanceled);
189	                return false;
190	            }

Tool reply2026-06-29T05:10:54.595Z
275	 <div class=journal-content>
276	 <div id=journal-34036-notes class="wiki journal-note" data-quote-reply-target=content><p>調査結果を中国語と日本語両方で報告します<br>↓↓↓↓↓↓↓↓↓↓中国語<br>■问题<br>　同一个商品，购买总数超过1000个时，取引正常。<br>　然后，对该取引进行指定明细打印收据的时候， 程序停止在当前画面，后续操作无反应。<br>■再现性<br>　可再现<br>■现在式样<br> 1.购物取引的商品数量变更时有999的上限<br> 2.指定明细打印收据的画面，右上有合计金额<br> 明细指定按下之前，取的是SalesHeader的TotalAmount<br> 明细指定按下之后，取的是指定明细的合计<br>■原因<br> 明细指定按下，原取引的商品明细复原<br> 商品数量不等于1的明细，则执行商品数量变更，<br> 在商品数量变更的逻辑中，同一个商品有一个最大数量的判断， 最大值不能超过999个。<br> 同一个商品的数量购买超过了999个，所以导致商品数量变更失败，并生成一个错误信息<br> 取引明细复原失败，商品明细行的数据集中没有数据，在画面更新获取总金额的时候，没有进行空指针判断，导致发生空指针异常<br>■方案<br> 1） 取引日志数据复原时， 删除最大商品数量判断的处理<br> 空指针问题也一起修改，明细复原失败的情况下用SalesHeader的TotalAmount<br> 2） 商品登录的数量变更逻辑改修，同一商品的件数上限确认<br> 影响：SSC和POS共通的商品登录式样，同一商品千件以上贩卖的时候只能分成多个小票了<br>■其他<br>部分取消的时候也有商品明细的复原处理<br>不同的是，没有空指针的问题，只会弹出异常消息不能进行部分取消。<br>所以我认为按上述方案1一起修改比较好。</p>
277	 <p>↓↓↓↓↓↓↓↓↓↓日本語<br>■問題<br>　同一商品、2回スキャンして1000件以上購入しました<br>　横領収書の指定発行する時、アイテム指定ボタン押下すると画面固まる<br>■再現性<br>　再現できます<br>■現行仕様<br>①売上取引に対して、<br>　数量変更する時、商品明細別件数上限は999があります<br>②アイテム指定領収証発行<br> 画面表示の右上にトータル金額(tran.Amount)があります<br> 　アイテム指定押下前に、SalesHeader.TotalAmount<br> 　アイテム指定押下後に、指定明細商品の合計<br>■問題発生原因<br>　アイテム指定ボタン押下すると、元取引の商品明細を復元します<br>　でも、1商品最大999個の制御があるので、明細商品の復元が失敗になって、エラーメッセージErrorItemQuantityMaxOverを追加<br>　画面リフレッシュして、指定商品の合計計算にNullReferenceエラーを発生しまい</p>
278	 <p>■対策案<br>案１）元取引の商品明細復元処理に、商品別件数上限制御をカットします<br> NullReference防止のためトータル金額計算ロジックにも改修して、商品明細復元失敗の場合SalesHeaderの金額を利用します。<br>案２）商品登録時、数量変更ロジックを改修して、商品明細別件数上限ではなく、同一商品の件数合計上限になります。<br>　　　影響：SSCとPOS共通商品登録ロジック、千件以上商品登録の場合複数レシートを分けていなければならない<br>■そのた<br>部分取消で商品明細復元処理もあります<br>NullReferenceのエラーがないですから、画面固まる問題がないです<br>でも、エラーPOPし、部分取消できていないの問題があります<br>一緒に案１）で改修したいと思います。</p></div>

Thinking

This is the smoking gun. The team's own bilingual root-cause analysis from 2023 (#4900 note #1) confirms EXACTLY the mechanism the workflow found:

Chinese (original team analysis): - 问题: 同一个商品购买总数超过1000个时取引正常。然后对该取引进行指定明细打印收据时，程序停止在当前画面，后续操作无反应。(Same product, when total purchased exceeds 1000, transaction is normal. But when printing specified-detail receipt, program stops/freezes on current screen.) - 现在式样: 购物取引的商品数量变更时有999的上限 (When changing item quantity, there's a 999 upper limit) - 原因: 明细指定按下，原取引的商品明细复原。商品数量不等于1的明细，则执行商品数量变更。在商品数量变更逻辑中，同一个商品有一个最大数量判断，最大值不能超过999个。同一商品数量购买超过999，导致商品数量变更失败并生成错误信息。取引明细复原失败，商品明细行数据集中没有数据，画面更新获取总金额时没进行空指针判断，导致空指针异常。 - 方案1: 取引日志数据复原时，删除最大商品数量判断的处理 - 其他: 部分取消时也有商品明细复原处理。不同的是没有空指针问题，只会弹出异常消息不能进行部分取消。

Japanese: - 同一商品、2回スキャンして1000件以上購入。横領収書の指定発行する時、アイテム指定ボタン押下すると画面固まる - 売上取引に対して数量変更する時、商品明細別件数上限は999 - アイテム指定ボタン押下すると、元取引の商品明細を復元。でも1商品最大999個の制御があるので、明細商品の復元が失敗になって、エラーメッセージErrorItemQuantityMaxOverを追加。画面リフレッシュして、指定商品の合計計算にNullReferenceエラーを発生 - 対策案1: 元取引の商品明細復元処理に、商品別件数上限制御をカット - 部分：部分取消で商品明細復元処理もあります。NullReferenceのエラーがないですから、画面固まる問題がないです。でもエラーPOPし、部分取消できていない。

This is 100% corroboration. The root cause is definitively: - Per-line-item quantity cap = 999 (LimitQuantity) enforced during transaction restore (ChangeQuantity → TryParseQuantity). - When a past transaction with a line of qty >= 1000 is restored (for 部分取消/部分返品 or アイテム指定領収証/#4900), the restore fails with ErrorItemQuantityMaxOver. - For #4900 (evidence receipt), additionally: failed restore → empty line items → tran.Amount getter → NullReferenceException → screen freeze. - For #6329 partial return: failed restore → ErrorItemQuantityMaxOver POP → cannot proceed.

And the ChangeQuantity routing is confirmed (LineItemBase.cs:180 → TryParseQuantity).

26	        }

32	        {
33	            get
34	            {
35	                return TranTypes.ReSales;
36	            }
37	        }

39	        /// <inheritdoc/>
40	        public override TranBase OperatingTran
41	        {
42	            get
43	            {
44	                if (this.ReSalesType == ReSalesTypes.Void || (this.VoidTran.CurrentState.IsOperating && !this.VoidTran.IsReSales) || this.VoidTran.CurrentState == PaymentStates.PaymentService_ConnectService)
45	                {
46	                    return this.VoidTran;
47	                }
48	
49	                return base.OperatingTran;
50	            }
51	        }
52	
53	        /// <summary>
54	        /// 取り消し取引
55	        /// </summary>
56	        public VoidTran VoidTran { get; private set; }
57

59	        /// 打ち直し種別

62	
63	        /// <summary>
64	        /// （計算）取消後成立でポイント
65	        /// </summary>
66	        public decimal CalPreReSalesPoint
67	        {
68	            get
69	            {
70	                if (!this.VoidTran.FixedTranLog.IsFixed)
71	                {
72	                    var totalAddPoint = 0m;
73	                    if (this.VoidTran.ReadReceiptObject.TranDataSet.Member.Count > 0)
74	                    {
75	                        var member = this.VoidTran.ReadReceiptObject.TranDataSet.Member[0];
76	                        totalAddPoint = !member.IsTotalAddPointNull() ? member.TotalAddPoint : 0m;
77	                    }
78	
79	                    var paymentPoint = this.VoidTran.PaymentObject.Items.Where(p => p.PaymentType == PaymentTypes.Point).Select(x => x.DepositAmount).Sum();
80	                    var result = this.MemberObject.BeforePoint + paymentPoint - totalAddPoint;
81	                    if (result < 0)
82	                    {
83	                        result = 0m;
84	                    }
85	
86	                    return result;
87	                }
88	                else
89	                {
90	                    return this.MemberObject.BeforePoint;
91	                }
92	            }
93	        }
94	
95	        /// <summary>
96	        /// （計算）取消成立後で電子マネー残高
97	        /// </summary>
98	        public int CalPreReSalesValueCardAmount
99	        {
100	            get
101	            {
102	                if (!this.VoidTran.FixedTranLog.IsFixed)
103	                {
104	                    var paymentObj = this.VoidTran.PaymentObject;
105	                    int valueCardAmount = (int)paymentObj.Items.Where(p => p.PaymentType == PaymentTypes.ValueCard).Select(x => x.PaymentAmount).Sum();
106	                    return valueCardAmount + this.MemberObject.ValueCardInquiryResult.DealResult.balanceTotal;
107	                }
108	                else
109	                {
110	                    return this.MemberObject.ValueCardInquiryResult.DealResult.balanceTotal;
111	                }
112	            }
113	        }

128	        /// </summary>
129	        /// <param name="input">入力値</param>
130	        /// <returns>True:成功、False:失敗</returns>
131	        public bool SetReason(string input)
132	        {
133	            // 部分取消の場合
134	            if (this.ReSalesType == ReSalesTypes.ReSales)
135	            {
136	                // 商品一つの部分取消の場合、理由が０８Pカードスキャン忘れしけない
137	                if (this.VoidTran.ReadReceiptObject.TranDataSet.LineItem.Where(l => !l.IsCanceled).Count() == 1 &&
138	                    this.VoidTran.ReadReceiptObject.TranDataSet.LineItem.Where(l => !l.IsCanceled).FirstOrDefault().Quantity == 1 &&
139	                    !input.Contains("08"))
140	                {
141	                    this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotReSalesDuetoItemCount);
142	                    return false;
143	                }
144	
145	                // 会員があるの場合、理由が０８Pカードスキャン忘れはだめです
146	                if (this.VoidTran.ReadReceiptObject.TranDataSet.Member.Count > 0 &&
147	                    input.Contains("08"))
148	                {
149	                    this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotReSalesDuetoReasonCode);
150	                    return false;
151	                }
152	            }
153	
154	            return this.VoidTran.SetReason(input);
155	        }
156

173	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
174	        public bool ScanReceiptBarcode(string inputData)
175	        {
176	            return this.VoidTran.ScanReceiptBarcode(inputData);
177	        }
178	
179	        /// <summary>
180	        /// 取引データセット読込み
181	        /// </summary>
182	        /// <param name="inputData">入力値</param>
183	        /// <returns>True:正常/False:異常</returns>
184	        public bool ReadTranDataSet(string inputData)
185	        {
186	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(inputData))
187	            {
188	                return false;
189	            }
190	
191	            string[] inputs = inputData.Split(',');
192	
193	            if (inputs.Length != 4)
194	            {
195	                return false;
196	            }
197	
198	            string reSalesTypeId = inputs[0];
199	            ReSalesType reSalesType;
200	
201	            if (reSalesTypeId == ReSalesTypes.Void.Id)
202	            {
203	                reSalesType = ReSalesTypes.Void;
204	            }
205	            else if (reSalesTypeId == ReSalesTypes.ReSales.Id)
206	            {
207	                reSalesType = ReSalesTypes.ReSales;
208	            }
209	            else
210	            {
211	                return false;
212	            }
213	
214	            if (!this.VoidTran.ReadTranDataSet(inputs[1], inputs[2], inputs[3], reSalesType))
215	            {
216	                return false;
217	            }
218	
219	            this.ReSalesType = reSalesType;
220

232	            {
233	                return false;
234	            }

253	            }
254	            else

256	                return false;
257	            }

260	            {
261	                return false;
262	            }
263	
264	            this.ReSalesType = reSalesType;
265	
266	            return true;
267	        }

269	        /// <summary>
270	        /// 小計処理
271	        /// </summary>
272	        /// <param name="inputData">入力値</param>
273	        /// <returns>true:正常/false:異常</returns>
274	        public new bool SubTotal(string inputData)
275	        {
276	            if (!this.SubTotalInternal(inputData))
277	            {
278	                return false;
279	            }

282	            {

284	                this.ManipulationTimes.RegistrationTime = (int)(this.ManipulationTimes.SubTotalDateTime.Value - this.VoidTran.GenerateDateTime.Value).TotalSeconds;
285	            }
286	
287	            this.CurrentState = SalesTranStates.Paying;
288	            this.ReCalcSalesTran();
289	
290	            return true;
291	        }

293	        /// <inheritdoc/>
294	        public override bool CancelTran()
295	        {
296	            if (this.VoidTran.CurrentState != VoidTranStates.Fixed)
297	            {
298	                if (!this.VoidTran.CancelTran())
299	                {
300	                    return false;
301	                }
302	
303	                // 取引中止の場合、取引ログはVoidTranで出力するのでReSalesでの取引中止ログは不要。
304	                this.CurrentState = SalesTranStates.Canceled;
305	
306	                // VoidEndTran後の取引中止の場合、MSRカード排出
307	                if (this.VoidTran.GenerateDateTime.HasValue && 
308	                    !this.VoidTran.MemberObject.HasMember &&
309	                    (this.MemberObject.HasMember || this.HasOfflinePointCardNo))
310	                {
311	                    MemberLibrary.TryEjectCard();
312	                }
313	
314	                return true;
315	            }
316	
317	            if (this.CurrentState == ReSalesTranStates.WaitingCancelTransactionCofirm)
318	            {
319	                var paymentObj = this.VoidTran.PaymentObject;
320	                var payment = paymentObj.Items.FirstOrDefault(p => !ReSalesLibrary.CanVoid(p.PaymentType.Code));
321	                if (payment != null)
322	                {
323	                    this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotVoidDuetoPayment, payment.Description);
324	                    return false;
325	                }
326	            }
327	
328	            return true;
329	        }

345	        /// <summary>
346	        /// VoidTranの確定処理
347	        /// </summary>
348	        /// <returns>true:正常/false:異常</returns>
349	        public bool VoidEndTran()
350	        {
351	            this.VoidTran.IsReSales = this.ReSalesType == ReSalesTypes.ReSales;
352	
353	            // 一括取消
354	            if (this.ReSalesType == ReSalesTypes.Void)
355	            {
356	                bool rsVoidTran = this.VoidTran.EndTran();
357	
358	                if (!rsVoidTran || this.VoidTran.CurrentState != VoidTranStates.Fixed)
359	                {
360	                    return rsVoidTran;
361	                }
362	
363	                this.CurrentState = SalesTranStates.Fixed;
364	                return true;
365	            }
366	
367	            // 部分取消
368	            else if (this.ReSalesType == ReSalesTypes.ReSales)
369	            {
370	                this.VoidTran.GenerateDateTime = DateTimeUtility.GetUserTimeNow();
371	
372	                // 保留した現金
373	                this.PaymentObject.TempDispenseChangeAmount = this.VoidTran.PaymentObject.TempDispenseChangeAmount;
374	                return this.RestoreReSales();
375	            }
376	
377	            return true;
378	        }
379	
380	        /// <summary>
381	        /// 取引終了処理
382	        /// </summary>
383	        /// <returns>true:正常/false:異常</returns>
384	        public override bool EndTran()
385	        {
386	            if (this.ReSalesType != ReSalesTypes.ReSales)
387	            {
388	                return false;
389	            }
390	
391	            if (!this.VoidTran.FixedTranLog.IsFixed)
392	            {
393	                // 取消取引終了処理
394	                bool rsVoidTran = this.VoidTran.EndTran();
395	
396	                if (!rsVoidTran || this.VoidTran.CurrentState != VoidTranStates.Fixed)
397	                {
398	                    // ポイント排他エラー時に現金支払いがあった場合は、現金支払いはキャンセルします。
399	                    var cashPayment = this.PaymentObject.Items.FirstOrDefault(p => p.PaymentType == PaymentTypes.Cash);
400	                    if (cashPayment != null)
401	                    {
402	                        this.PaymentObject.CancelPayment(cashPayment.KeyNo);
403	                    }
404	
405	                    return rsVoidTran;
406	                }
407	
408	                if (this.MemberObject.HasMember)
409	                {
410	                    // 会員情報更新
411	                    if (this.VoidTran.PaymentObject.HasPayments && 
412	                        this.VoidTran.PaymentObject.Items.Any(p => p.PaymentType == PaymentTypes.ValueCard))
413	                    {
414	                        var payment = this.VoidTran.PaymentObject.Items.Where(p => p.PaymentType == PaymentTypes.ValueCard).FirstOrDefault() as PaymentValueCard;
415	                        this.MemberObject.ValueCardInquiryResult.DealResult.balanceTotal = payment.ValueCardExecuteResult.DealResult.balanceTotal;
416	                    }
417	
418	                    this.MemberObject.PointInquiryResult.BeforePoint = this.VoidTran.MemberObject.BeforePoint;
419	                }
420	            }
421	            
422	            if (base.EndTran())
423	            {
424	                var printInfos = this.UserData.GetRJPrintInfoStore();
425	                this.UserData.GetRJPrintInfoStore().SetTempPrintInfos(this.ReceiptPrintData);
426	                printInfos.CanPrint = true;
427	                return true;
428	            }
429	
430	            return false;
431	        }

455	            {

457	                if (!lineItemData.IsCanceled)
458	                {
459	                    if (lineItemNo == lineItem.KeyNo)
460	                    {
461	                        if (lineItemData.Quantity < quantity)
462	                        {
463	                            this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotAddQuantity);
464	                            return false;
465	                        }
466	
467	                        break;
468	                    }
469	
470	                    lineItemNo++;
471	                }
472	            }
473	
474	            return true;
475	        }

477	        /// <summary>
478	        /// モバイルから呼び出される一括取消のパラメータをチェックします。
479	        /// </summary>
480	        /// <param name="pointCartNo">会員番号</param>
481	        /// <param name="reasonCode">理由コード</param>
482	        /// <returns>True:エラーなし/False:エラーあり</returns>
483	        public bool CheckVoid4MobileParam(string pointCartNo, string reasonCode)
484	        {

486	            {
487	                // 取引が存在しないため取消不可
488	                // (実際は取引自体は存在するが、別の会員が悪意を持って取り消そうとする可能性もある為、会員違いは取引無しのメッセージを返す)
489	                this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorNotFoundSalesTransaction);
490	                this.VoidTran.CurrentState = VoidTranStates.Neutral;
491	                this.CurrentState = SalesTranStates.Canceled;
492	                return false;
493	            }
494	
495	            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(reasonCode))
496	            {
497	                this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorRequiredWithName, "ReasonCode");
498	                this.VoidTran.CurrentState = VoidTranStates.Neutral;
499	                this.CurrentState = SalesTranStates.Canceled;
500	                return false;
501	            }
502	            else if (reasonCode.Length != 3)
503	            {
504	                this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorInvalidReasonCode, reasonCode);
505	                this.VoidTran.CurrentState = VoidTranStates.Neutral;
506	                this.CurrentState = SalesTranStates.Canceled;
507	                return false;
508	            }
509	            else
510	            {
511	                var reasonRow = ReasonMasterAccessor.GetReasonCodeMasterRow(this.UserData.DataAccessParameter, reasonCode.Substring(0, 1), reasonCode.Substring(1, 2));
512	
513	                if (reasonRow == null || reasonRow.Length == 0)
514	                {
515	                    this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorInvalidReasonCode, reasonCode);
516	                    this.VoidTran.CurrentState = VoidTranStates.Neutral;
517	                    this.CurrentState = SalesTranStates.Canceled;
518	                    return false;
519	                }
520	                else
521	                {
522	                    this.SetReasonWithType(reasonCode.Substring(0, 1), reasonRow[0].ReasonCode, reasonRow[0].Description);
523	                }
524	            }
525	
526	            return true;
527	        }

Now let me write the final comprehensive analysis. I have everything verified:

  1. Root cause (CONFIRMED by code + team's own 2023 analysis):
  2. SettingValues.LimitQuantity = 999 (商品数量の最大値)
  3. During restore (RestoreTranObject.RestoreLineItems), each line with Quantity != 1 is re-applied via ChangeQuantity → TryParseQuantity, which rejects quantity > 999 (ErrorItemQuantityMaxOver, code 1809).

  4. A single line with qty >= 1000 fails to restore → aborts.

  5. Why #6329 fails: 部分返品 (打ち直し) calls RestoreReSales → RestoreLineItems → 999 cap → ErrorItemQuantityMaxOver POP → cannot proceed. (全返品/Void reads SalesHeader totals directly via VoidTran.EndTran and does NOT restore, so it likely does NOT hit this cap — see nuance.)

  6. Why #4900 fails (same root cause): EvidenceReceipt RestoreEvidenceItems → RestoreEvidenceTran → RestoreLineItems → 999 cap → restore fails → live LineItems empty → EvidenceReceiptTran.Amount dereferences .FirstOrDefault().Value → NullReferenceException → screen freezes.

  7. Why レシート無し返品 (receipt-less return) works: It does NOT load/restore a past transaction; items are re-entered fresh via PLU scan, so the stored large quantity never passes through ChangeQuantity/TryParseQuantity.

  8. The boundary is exactly 1000 because the cap is 999 (off-by-one). No literal "1000" in code.

  9. Decided fix = BLOCK (not fix the cap): Per #6329 note #16 / #4900 note #28, block with yellow POP.

  10. Guard location: VoidTran.LocalReadTranDataSet (and ReadTranDataSet) after TransactionType/mode validation, before reSalesType branches. Check tranDs.LineItem.Any(l => !l.IsCanceled && l.Quantity >= 1000). Add MessageId + Message.xml/Message_FC.xml entry. SetError auto-renders yellow POP.

  11. Rejected hypotheses: Storage/serialization (NVARCHAR(MAX), Int32 — no limit at 1000); arithmetic overflow/precision (decimal division — no overflow, secondary at most); printer/EJ buffer (no such limit).

  12. Open questions:

  13. Does 全返品 (full Void) actually fail? Code says no (reads totals directly). The block covers it anyway per decision.
  14. >= 1000 vs > LimitQuantity.Value — equivalent today; tie to setting for robustness, but message hardcodes "1000".
  15. Verify new message's MessageTypeId is Warning (yellow).

  16. 4900 is out of decided scope but shares cause.

Let me also reconsider the team's note: the 2023 team proposed 案1 (remove the cap during restore — actual fix) and 案2 (change to total-count limit). But the 2026 decision is to BLOCK instead. So the current design (#6329/#4900) chose the BLOCK approach over the 2023 fix proposals. I'll mention this.

#6329 代码分析报告：1明细1000个以上的交易无法退货

一、问题与时间线（来自 Redmine）

核心事实：同一商品购买 1000 个以上的交易，在「1个月以内的退货」功能中报错无法使用；「无收据退货」可作为变通方案。关联问题 #4900（横向收据的指定发行）也有相同症状，二者共用同一根本原因。

时间 事件
2024/04/26 #6329 立项（bug）
2026/05/27 店铺支持决定采用运营规避 → 改为「禁止退货」方针
2026/06/05 设计草案（对象：1个月以内的退货、部分退货；黄色POP提示）
2026/06/09 最终设计确定（状态：进行中，进度＝1 设计）。最终消息：1商品1000点以上購入を含むレシートは「1か月以内の返品」はできません。「返品」から返品を行ってください。

注：#4900 中 2023 年团队就已在注释里用中日双语写明了根因分析（见下文），与本次代码分析的结论完全一致。

二、根本原因（已通过代码 + 团队自身分析双重验证）

核心机制：每行明细数量上限 = 999

SettingValues.cs:38 定义了「商品数量的最大值」：

public static SettingValue<int> LimitQuantity { get; } = new SettingValue<int>(nameof(LimitQuantity), 999);

触发点：还原过去的交易时，对每行重新应用数量

读取过去交易后，RestoreTranObject.cs:618-625 的 RestoreLineItems 会对每一行 Quantity != 1 的明细，重新调用 ChangeQuantity：

if (lineItemRow.Quantity != 1)
{
    // 数量変更
    if (!tran.ChangeLineItem<LineItemBase>(lineItem, l => l.ChangeQuantity(tran, lineItemRow.Quantity.ToString())))
        return false;
}

LineItemBase.cs:180 的 ChangeQuantity → LineItemCommonLogic.cs:71-75 的 TryParseQuantity 执行上限校验：

// 最大値チェック
if (quantity > SettingValues.LimitQuantity.Value)   // 999
{
    tran.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorItemQuantityMaxOver);   // Code 1809
    return false;
}

结论：单个明细数量 ≥ 1000 时，还原在 > 999 处失败 → ErrorItemQuantityMaxOver → 整个还原中止。 边界恰好是 1000，因为上限是 999（off-by-one），代码中并没有字面量 1000。

三、为何三个症状各自表现不同（同一根因）

现象 路径 表现
#6329 部分退货失败 ReSalesTran.VoidEndTran（ReSalesTran.cs:368-375）→ RestoreReSales() → RestoreLineItems 弹出 ErrorItemQuantityMaxOver，无法进行部分退货
#4900 画面卡死 EvidenceReceiptTran.RestoreEvidenceItems → RestoreEvidenceTran → RestoreLineItems 还原失败→活跃明细集合为空→EvidenceReceiptTran.cs:86 Amount 取 .FirstOrDefault().Value → NullReferenceException → 画面冻结
无收据退货可规避 不读取/还原过去交易，商品通过 PLU 扫描重新录入 大数量不经过 ChangeQuantity/TryParseQuantity，不触发 999 上限

团队 2023 年在 #4900 的注释原话：「1商品最大999個の制御があるので、明細商品の復元が失敗になって…ErrorItemQuantityMaxOver…NullReferenceエラー」「部分取消…NullReferenceのエラーがないですから…でもエラーPOPし、部分取消できていない」——与上述完全吻合。

四、关于「全额退货（全返品）」的重要补充（诚实说明）

ReSalesTran.VoidEndTran 按取消类型分流： - 全返品（Void） → VoidTran.EndTran()，直接读取 SalesHeader 合计，不调用 RestoreLineItems → 理论上不会触发 999 上限。 - 部分返品（ReSales） → RestoreReSales() → 触发上限 → 失败。

因此：999 上限机制确定性地解释了「部分退货」与 #4900 的失败；而 #6329 描述中笼统的「1个月以内退货报错」，实际失败点最可能是其中的部分退货路径。全返品是否真的失败，需要在实机上复现确认（见开放问题）。但无论如何，团队决定的拦截（POP）方案覆盖两种范围，guard 的插入位置不受影响。

五、设计阶段需要落地的「禁止 + 黄色POP」（建议的代码位置）

因当前决定是「禁止而非修复」，建议在读取交易后、选择取消类型之前拦截。两个范围（全返品/部分退货）都经由 VoidTran 汇聚，所以一处 guard 即可覆盖两者。

① 主拦截点 — VoidTran.cs 的 LocalReadTranDataSet（WinPOS 实际调用的路径，UI 经 ScanReceiptBarcodeDialog → ReSales_ReadTranDataSet → ReSalesTran.LocalReadTranDataSet → VoidTran.LocalReadTranDataSet）： 在交易类型/模式校验之后（约 L314 之后）、各支付校验之前插入，沿用现有 L415-436 的同类拦截写法：

if (tranDs.LineItem.Any(l => !l.IsCanceled && l.Quantity >= 1000))
{
    this.SetError(MessageIds.ErrorCannotReSalesDuetoQuantityOver1000); // 新增 MessageId
    return false;
}

② 防御性第二点 — 同文件 ReadTranDataSet（L188，LogicService/移动端 Void4Mobile 可能使用），在 L217 后、SelectReason（L272）前同样插入。

③ 消息接线（无需改 UI/Command，SetError 经 MessageDialogInfoCreator.CreateWarningDialogInfo 自动渲染为黄色 POP）： - MessageIds.cs：在 ErrorCannotReSalesDuetoItemCount（L1526）旁新增同名 MessageId。 - Message.xml 与 Message_FC.xml 同步新增（仿 Code 4011，取空闲 Code 4025）： <Message Id="ErrorCannotReSalesDuetoQuantityOver1000" Code="4025">1商品1000点以上購入を含むレシートは「1か月以内の返品」はできません。&#xa;「返品」から返品を行ってください。</Message>

不要改 RestoreTranObject（为时已晚，还原已中止），也不要放进 #4900 的 EvidenceReceiptTran（当前决定范围外，但根因相同）。

六、被排除的假设

  • 存储/序列化：TransactionData 是 NVARCHAR(MAX)（maxLength=2147483647），Quantity 是 Int32，1000 个数量几乎不增加 XML 体积，字节级完全一致——排除。
  • 算术溢出/除零：Amount/TaxDivided/UnitPrice 均为 decimal，Quantity 为 int，Amount/Quantity 是 decimal 运算，不会溢出或除零——最多是 #4900 金额的次要精度问题，非硬性 1000 卡点。
  • 打印机/电子日志缓冲：无此类限制——排除。

七、开放问题（需在实机确认，因为本次禁止编译/执行）

  1. 全返品是否真的失败？ 代码显示全返品不调用还原、不触发 999 上限。需复现确认；若不失败，禁止方案仍按设计覆盖它，但理由与部分退货不同。
  2. 判定用 >= 1000 还是 > LimitQuantity.Value？ 当下等价（999）；若主数据改了 LimitQuantity，硬编码 1000 会与实际上限脱节。建议用 > LimitQuantity.Value 跟踪真实上限（注意消息文案硬编码了「1000点以上」）。
  3. 新消息的 MessageTypeId 是否为 Warning（黄色）？ 需核对 ErrorCannotReSalesDuetoItemCount 的渲染类型，确保是黄色 POP。
  4. #4900 是否一并处理？ 共享同一根因，但当前设计明确只拦截「1个月以内退货 + 部分退货」，#4900 的 NullRef 卡死是否同时修，需团队决定。

如需，我可以继续：把上述 guard + 消息的具体 diff 写出来（仅编辑代码，不编译），或针对「全返品是否失败」这个开放问题进一步深入追踪打印/积分链路。

The loader:

param([string]$Data = "...\data.json")
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
[System.Threading.Thread]::CurrentThread.CurrentCulture = 'en-US'
$json = Get-Content -Path $Data -Encoding UTF8 -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
$excel = New-Object -ComObject Excel.Application
$excel.Visible = $false
$excel.DisplayAlerts = $false
$wb = $excel.Workbooks.Add()
# remove extra sheets
while ($wb.Worksheets.Count -gt 1) { $wb.Worksheets.Item($wb.Worksheets.Count).Delete() }
$first = $true
foreach ($s in $json.sheets) {
    if ($first) { $ws = $wb.Worksheets.Item(1); $first=$false } else { $ws = $wb.Worksheets.Add([System.Reflection.Missing]::Value, $wb.Worksheets.Item($wb.Worksheets.Count)) }
    ... build ...
}
$wb.SaveAs($json.output, 51)
$wb.Close($false)
$excel.Quit()
# cleanup

Wait, Worksheets.Add syntax: $ws = $wb.Worksheets.Add($after?) — In COM, Add(Before, After, Count, Type). To add after the last sheet: $wb.Worksheets.Add($null, $lastSheet). Passing $null for Before and a sheet for After. In PS COM, use [System.Reflection.Missing]::Value for omitted args, or pass $null. Let me use: $ws = $wb.Worksheets.Add([System.Reflection.Missing]::Value, $lastSheet). Actually simpler: just add and don't worry about order, or rename. Order doesn't strictly matter but nice to have. Let me add after the last.

Hmm, COM optional args in PS 5.1: you can pass [System.Reflection.Missing]::Value. Let me do that.

Cell writing:

$ws.Name = $s.name

# title
$ws.Cells.Item(1,1).Value2 = $s.title
$ws.Range($ws.Cells.Item(1,1), $ws.Cells.Item(1,$ncols)).Merge() | Out-Null
# headers at row 2
for ($c=0; $c -lt $ncols; $c++) { $ws.Cells.Item(2, $c+1).Value2 = $s.headers[$c] }
# data
$r = 3
foreach ($row in $s.rows) {
  for ($c=0; $c -lt $row.Count; $c++) {
    $val = $row[$c]
    if ($null -ne $val) { $ws.Cells.Item($r, $c+1).Value2 = $val.ToString() }
  }
  $r++
}

Let me write the loader:

param(
  [string]$DataPath = "d:\POJ\NoteType\tasks\6329\_report_data.json"
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
# Excel COM locale guard
$ci = New-Object System.Globalization.CultureInfo('en-US')
[System.Threading.Thread]::CurrentThread.CurrentCulture = $ci
[System.Threading.Thread]::CurrentThread.CurrentUICulture = $ci

if (-not (Test-Path $DataPath)) { throw "data file not found: $DataPath" }
$data = Get-Content -Path $DataPath -Encoding UTF8 -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json

$outDir = Split-Path $data.output -Parent
if (-not (Test-Path $outDir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $outDir | Out-Null }

$excel = New-Object -ComObject Excel.Application
$excel.Visible = $false
$excel.DisplayAlerts = $false
try {
  $wb = $excel.Workbooks.Add()
  # ensure single starting sheet
  while ($wb.Worksheets.Count -gt 1) { $wb.Worksheets.Item($wb.Worksheets.Count).Delete() }

  $sheetIndex = 0
  foreach ($s in $data.sheets) {
    if ($sheetIndex -eq 0) {
      $ws = $wb.Worksheets.Item(1)
    } else {
      $last = $wb.Worksheets.Item($wb.Worksheets.Count)
      $ws = $wb.Worksheets.Add([System.Reflection.Missing]::Value, $last)
    }
    $sheetIndex++

    $ws.Name = $s.name
    $ncols = $s.headers.Count
    # text format for columns
    for ($c = 1; $c -le $ncols; $c++) { $ws.Columns.Item($c).NumberFormat = "@" }

    # title
    $ws.Cells.Item(1,1).Value2 = $s.title
    $ws.Range($ws.Cells.Item(1,1), $ws.Cells.Item(1,$ncols)).Merge() | Out-Null
    $ws.Cells.Item(1,1).Font.Size = 13
    $ws.Cells.Item(1,1).Font.Bold = $true
    $ws.Cells.Item(1,1).Interior.Color = 15921906  # light gray
    $ws.Rows.Item(1).RowHeight = 26

    # headers (row 2)
    for ($c = 0; $c -lt $ncols; $c++) { $ws.Cells.Item(2, ($c+1)).Value2 = [string]$s.headers[$c] }
    $hdr = $ws.Range($ws.Cells.Item(2,1), $ws.Cells.Item(2,$ncols))
    $hdr.Interior.Color = 6968388   # dark blue
    $hdr.Font.Color = 16777215      # white
    $hdr.Font.Bold = $true
    $hdr.HorizontalAlignment = -4108 # center
    $hdr.VerticalAlignment = -4108
    $ws.Rows.Item(2).RowHeight = 20

    # data
    $r = 3
    foreach ($row in $s.rows) {
      $cellCount = $row.Count
      if ($cellCount -gt $ncols) { $cellCount = $ncols }
      for ($c = 0; $c -lt $cellCount; $c++) {
        $v = $row[$c]
        if ($null -ne $v) { $ws.Cells.Item($r, ($c+1)).Value2 = [string]$v }
      }
      $r++
    }
    $lastRow = $r - 1

    # styling data area
    if ($lastRow -ge 3) {
      $body = $ws.Range($ws.Cells.Item(3,1), $ws.Cells.Item($lastRow,$ncols))
      $body.WrapText = $true
      $body.VerticalAlignment = -4160  # top
    }
    $all = $ws.Range($ws.Cells.Item(2,1), $ws.Cells.Item($lastRow,$ncols))
    $all.Borders.LineStyle = 1
    $all.Borders.Weight = 2

    # widths
    for ($c = 0; $c -lt $s.widths.Count; $c++) { $ws.Columns.Item($c+1).ColumnWidth = [double]$s.widths[$c] }

    # freeze
    try { $ws.Activate(); $ws.Cells.Item(3,1).Select() | Out-Null; $excel.ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = $true } catch {}

    [System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($ws) | Out-Null
  }

  $wb.Worksheets.Item(1).Activate()
  $wb.SaveAs($data.output, 51)
  $wb.Close($false)
  [System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($wb) | Out-Null
}
finally {
  $excel.Quit()
  [System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($excel) | Out-Null
  [GC]::Collect()
  [GC]::WaitForPendingFinalizers()
}

$out = Get-Item $data.output
"WROTE: $($out.FullName)"
"SIZE:  $($out.Length) bytes"
"SHEETS: $($data.sheets.Count)"

Wait — there's an issue: I release $ws inside the loop, then use $last = $wb.Worksheets.Item(...) at top of next iteration which is fine (gets from wb). Also $ws.Cells.Item(3,1).Select() requires the sheet active and window; in non-visible Excel, Select can throw. The try/catch covers it.

Also a subtlety: after Merge of row 1, $ws.Cells.Item(1,1) is the merged cell; fine.

Borders: LineStyle=1 (xlContinuous), Weight=2 (xlThin). Good.

ColumnWidth as [double] — fine.
